Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the recognition object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 592. The moduli space has dimension at most 746 with 4 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_592(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 592. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^746(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Statement: Every optimality condition deformation of the recognition object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 510. The moduli space has dimension at most 111 with 5 connected components.

For the inductive step, we decompose the recognition structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 111. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Proof: The entropy bound estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 75 + O(1/n^192). Summing over all modes and applying the recognition entropy bound criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
